- AC-1 no global, no project cap -> 5. AC-2 global 7, no project -> 7. AC-3 global 7, project 2 -> 2.
- AC-4 verificationIteration == effective cap on reject -> needs_human iterations_exhausted. AC-5 below cap -> back to in_progress with iteration incremented.
- AC-6 delivery: project no cap, global 2, third conflict escalates with delivery_failed.
- AC-7 PUT {maxVerificationIterations: 8} saves, GET returns it, settings.updated recorded once. AC-8 same value -> no write, no event.
- AC-9 PUT 0, 21, decimal, string or unknown field -> 400 invalid_input, stored value unchanged. AC-10 GET without saved -> 5.
- AC-11 non-local Host/Origin or non-loopback socket -> 403 forbidden_origin for GET and PUT.
- AC-12 Settings page: valid value saves and rereads; invalid (empty, 0, decimal, >20) sends nothing and shows field message; read/save errors show role=alert; ES and EN texts.
- AC-13 old settings.updated payloads (providers or globalConcurrency only) remain valid; payload with only maxVerificationIterations valid.
- AC-14 stored invalid global value -> default 5.
- AC-15 resumed task after iterations_exhausted with resetIteration gets a full cycle of effective-cap reworks.
- Invariants: effective cap integer 1..20; project cap overrides global; saving the same global value writes nothing; changing global never interrupts Runs in progress.

Your job: code quality, design, compliance with the spec above, and coherence of the tests against the criteria. Specifically check: the test coherence checklist (each criterion has a test named in domain language; tests check observable behavior; no skipped tests, empty or trivial assertions; invariants and edge cases covered; no existing test weakened, e.g. the default 3->5 updates in existing tests should be pure default changes, not relaxed assertions). Task: verification iteration cap configurable globally (Settings, key 'verification' in app_settings, GET/PUT /api/settings/verification) and per project. Effective cap = project limits.maxVerificationIterations > global saved > DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS=5. Integer 1-20. Idempotent save (same value: no write, no event). settings.updated carries maxVerificationIterations. Invalid stored value falls back to 5. Verdict reject and delivery conflict cap use the effective cap. Settings page section with ES/EN texts.

AC-1 no global, project without cap -> 5. AC-2 global 7, project none -> 7. AC-3 global 7, project 2 -> 2. AC-4 verificationIteration == effective cap on reject -> needs_human iterations_exhausted. AC-5 below cap -> in_progress, iteration incremented. AC-6 global 2, delivery third conflict -> delivery_failed. AC-7 PUT 8 from localhost returns 8, GET 8, one settings.updated. AC-11 non-local Host/Origin or non-loopback socket -> 403 forbidden_origin. AC-12 Settings page: valid save and reread; invalid (empty,0,decimal,>20) sends nothing and shows field message; read/save errors show role=alert; ES and EN texts. AC-13 old settings.updated payloads remain valid; payload with only maxVerificationIterations valid. AC-14 invalid stored value -> 5. AC-15 resumed task after iterations_exhausted with resetIteration gets a full cycle of effective-cap reworks.

Review for quality, design, adherence to the spec, and coherence of the tests against the checklist (each criterion has a domain-named test; observable behavior; no skipped/empty/trivial assertions; invariants and edge cases covered; no weakened existing test). Also check architecture rules in AGENTS.md (domain depends only on schemas; English code/tests; UI bilingual via dictionaries).

Report ONLY findings, one per line: `[<severity>] <file>[:<line>] · <criterion> · <evidence>` with severity critical|major|minor, or the single line `NO FINDINGS`. Verify each finding by reading the code. Be concise.

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { en } from "./en.ts";
import { es } from "./es.ts";

/** Dotted path of every leaf (string or function) of a dictionary. */
function leaves(node: unknown, prefix = ""): [string, unknown][] {
  if (typeof node === "string" || typeof node === "function") return [[prefix, node]];
  return Object.entries(node as Record<string, unknown>).flatMap(([key, value]) =>
    leaves(value, prefix === "" ? key : `${prefix}.${key}`),
  );
}

describe("dictionaries", () => {
  it("English has exactly the same keys as Spanish", () => {
    expect(leaves(en).map(([path]) => path)).toEqual(leaves(es).map(([path]) => path));
  });

  it("no value is empty", () => {
    for (const dictionary of [es, en]) {
      for (const [path, value] of leaves(dictionary)) {
        if (typeof value === "string") expect(value.trim(), path).not.toBe("");
      }
    }
  });

  it("parameterized texts keep the same arity in both languages", () => {
    const english = new Map(leaves(en));
    for (const [path, value] of leaves(es)) {
      if (typeof value === "function") {
        expect((english.get(path) as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown).length, path).toBe(
          value.length,
        );
      }
    }
  });
});
```
